src/game/audio.ts:  private loopSottofondoMistero2() {
src/game/audio.ts:    const loopLength = 8 / this.musicSpeed;
src/game/audio.ts:    let nextLoopTime = this.ctx!.currentTime;
src/game/audio.ts:
src/game/audio.ts:    const playLoop = () => {
src/game/audio.ts:        if (!this.ctx || !this.enabled || !this.musicGain || this.currentTrackId !== 'sottofindomistero2') return;
src/game/audio.ts:        
src/game/audio.ts:        if (nextLoopTime < this.ctx.currentTime) nextLoopTime = this.ctx.currentTime;
src/game/audio.ts:        const now = nextLoopTime;
src/game/audio.ts:
src/game/audio.ts:        // Eerie, haunting tones
src/game/audio.ts:        const baseFreqs = [146.83, 110.00, 138.59, 92.50]; // D, A, C#, F#
src/game/audio.ts:        
src/game/audio.ts:        baseFreqs.forEach((freq, idx) => {
src/game/audio.ts:            const start = now + (idx * 2) / this.musicSpeed;
src/game/audio.ts:            
src/game/audio.ts:            // Slow, sweeping pad
src/game/audio.ts:            const osc = this.ctx!.createOscillator();
src/game/audio.ts:            const gain = this.ctx!.createGain();
src/game/audio.ts:            osc.type = 'sine';
src/game/audio.ts:            osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, start);
src/game/audio.ts:            osc.frequency.linearRampToValueAtTime(freq * 1.01, start + 2 / this.musicSpeed);
src/game/audio.ts:            
src/game/audio.ts:            gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0, start);
src/game/audio.ts:            gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.04, start + 0.8 / this.musicSpeed);
src/game/audio.ts:            gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0, start + 2.0 / this.musicSpeed);
src/game/audio.ts:            
src/game/audio.ts:            osc.connect(gain);
src/game/audio.ts:            gain.connect(this.musicGain!);
src/game/audio.ts:            osc.start(start);
src/game/audio.ts:            osc.stop(start + 2.0 / this.musicSpeed);

src/game/audio.ts:  private loopSottofondoMistero1() {
src/game/audio.ts:    const loopLength = 8 / this.musicSpeed; // 8 seconds per loop
src/game/audio.ts:    let nextLoopTime = this.ctx!.currentTime;
src/game/audio.ts:
src/game/audio.ts:    const playLoop = () => {
src/game/audio.ts:        if (!this.ctx || !this.enabled || !this.musicGain || this.currentTrackId !== 'sottofindomistero1') return;
src/game/audio.ts:        
src/game/audio.ts:        // Re-sync nextLoopTime with currentTime if it fell behind (e.g. tab backgrounded)
src/game/audio.ts:        if (nextLoopTime < this.ctx.currentTime) {
src/game/audio.ts:            nextLoopTime = this.ctx.currentTime;
src/game/audio.ts:        }
src/game/audio.ts:        
src/game/audio.ts:        const now = nextLoopTime;
src/game/audio.ts:
src/game/audio.ts:        // Simple 4-chord progression
src/game/audio.ts:        const notes = [
src/game/audio.ts:            [130.81, 155.56, 196.00, 261.63], // Cm
src/game/audio.ts:            [103.83, 130.81, 155.56, 207.65], // Ab
src/game/audio.ts:            [155.56, 196.00, 233.08, 311.13], // Eb
src/game/audio.ts:            [146.83, 196.00, 246.94, 293.66]  // G
src/game/audio.ts:        ];
src/game/audio.ts:
src/game/audio.ts:        notes.forEach((chord, chordIdx) => {
src/game/audio.ts:            // Arpeggio
src/game/audio.ts:            chord.forEach((freq, noteIdx) => {
src/game/audio.ts:                const osc = this.ctx!.createOscillator();
src/game/audio.ts:                const gain = this.ctx!.createGain();
src/game/audio.ts:                
src/game/audio.ts:                osc.type = 'sine';
src/game/audio.ts:                osc.frequency.value = freq * 2; // an octave higher usually sounds better for arps
src/game/audio.ts:                
src/game/audio.ts:                
src/game/audio.ts:                
src/game/audio.ts:                osc.connect(gain);
src/game/audio.ts:                gain.connect(this.musicGain!);
src/game/audio.ts:                
src/game/audio.ts:            });
src/game/audio.ts:            
src/game/audio.ts:            // Bass pad
src/game/audio.ts:            const bassOsc = this.ctx!.createOscillator();
src/game/audio.ts:            const bassGain = this.ctx!.createGain();
src/game/audio.ts:            bassOsc.type = 'triangle';
src/game/audio.ts:            bassOsc.frequency.value = chord[0] / 2; // octave down
src/game/audio.ts:            
src/game/audio.ts:            const bassStart = now + (chordIdx * 2) / this.musicSpeed;
src/game/audio.ts:            bassGain.gain.setValueAtTime(0, bassStart);
src/game/audio.ts:            bassGain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.06, bassStart + 0.2 / this.musicSpeed);
src/game/audio.ts:            bassGain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.03, bassStart + 1.8 / this.musicSpeed);
src/game/audio.ts:            bassGain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0, bassStart + 2.0 / this.musicSpeed);
src/game/audio.ts:            
src/game/audio.ts:            bassOsc.connect(bassGain);
src/game/audio.ts:            bassGain.connect(this.musicGain!);
src/game/audio.ts:            
src/game/audio.ts:            bassOsc.start(bassStart);
src/game/audio.ts:            bassOsc.stop(bassStart + 2.0 / this.musicSpeed);
src/game/audio.ts:        });
src/game/audio.ts:
src/game/audio.ts:        nextLoopTime += loopLength;
src/game/audio.ts:        // Schedule next playLoop slightly before the current one ends
src/game/audio.ts:        const timeToNextLoop = (nextLoopTime - this.ctx.currentTime - 0.1 / this.musicSpeed) * 1000;
src/game/audio.ts:        this.musicTimeout = setTimeout(playLoop, Math.max(0, timeToNextLoop));
src/game/audio.ts:    };
src/game/audio.ts:
src/game/audio.ts:    playLoop();
src/game/audio.ts:  }

src/components/ShopUI.tsx:function selectShopItems(): ShopItem[] {
src/components/ShopUI.tsx:    const selected: ShopItem[] = [];
src/components/ShopUI.tsx:    const potionCount = Math.random() < 0.5 ? 1 : 2;
src/components/ShopUI.tsx:    for(let i=0; i<potionCount; i++) selected.push(POTIONS[Math.floor(Math.random() * POTIONS.length)]);
src/components/ShopUI.tsx:    selected.push(WEAPONS_PHYS[Math.floor(Math.random() * WEAPONS_PHYS.length)]);
src/components/ShopUI.tsx:    selected.push(WEAPONS_MAG[Math.floor(Math.random() * WEAPONS_MAG.length)]);
src/components/ShopUI.tsx:    selected.push(CRYSTALS[Math.floor(Math.random() * CRYSTALS.length)]);
src/components/ShopUI.tsx:    selected.push(XP_ITEMS[Math.floor(Math.random() * XP_ITEMS.length)]);
src/components/ShopUI.tsx:    selected.push(MIDA_ITEMS[Math.floor(Math.random() * MIDA_ITEMS.length)]);
src/components/ShopUI.tsx:    selected.push(MANA_REGEN_ITEMS[Math.floor(Math.random() * MANA_REGEN_ITEMS.length)]);
src/components/ShopUI.tsx:    if (Math.random() < 0.5) selected.push(CHESTS[Math.floor(Math.random() * CHESTS.length)]);
src/components/ShopUI.tsx:    const uniqueItems = Array.from(new Map(selected.map(item => [item.id, item])).values());
src/components/ShopUI.tsx:    return uniqueItems.sort((a,b) => a.price - b.price);
src/components/ShopUI.tsx:}

src/components/ShopUI.tsx:

src/components/ShopUI.tsx:            else if (item.name === 'MP Charge') stats.current.mp = Math.min(stats.current.maxMp, stats.current.mp + 50);
src/components/ShopUI.tsx:            else if (item.name === 'Heavy Module') {
src/components/ShopUI.tsx:                const weaponNames = Object.keys(WEAPONS).filter(k => WEAPONS[k].type === 'sword' || WEAPONS[k].type === 'hammer' || WEAPONS[k].type === 'boomerang');

src/components/ShopUI.tsx:                if (stats.current.physicalWeapon === picked) {

src/components/ShopUI.tsx:                } else {
src/components/ShopUI.tsx:                    stats.current.physicalWeapon = picked;
src/components/ShopUI.tsx:                    stats.current.physicalStacks = 1;
src/components/ShopUI.tsx:                }
src/components/ShopUI.tsx:            }
src/components/ShopUI.tsx:            else if (item.name === 'Arcane Core') {

src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:import React from 'react';
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:import { motion } from 'motion/react';
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:import { Heart, Zap, Sword, Shield, Wind, Sparkles, X } from 'lucide-react';
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:interface Props {
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:    stats: React.MutableRefObject<any>;
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:    onClose: () => void;
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:    lang: 'it' | 'en';
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:}
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:const SKILL_COSTS = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5]; // up to 5 levels per stat
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:export default function SkillTreeUI({ stats, onClose, lang }: Props) {
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:    const s = stats.current;
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:    // ensure skill properties exist
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:    if (s.skillPoints === undefined) s.skillPoints = 0;
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:    if (s.hpLevel === undefined) s.hpLevel = 0;
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:    if (s.mpLevel === undefined) s.mpLevel = 0;
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:    if (s.strLevel === undefined) s.strLevel = 0;
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:    if (s.mpRegenLevel === undefined) s.mpRegenLevel = 0;
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:    if (s.speedLevel === undefined) s.speedLevel = 0;
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:    const [, setTick] = React.useState(0);
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:    const forceUpdate = () => setTick(t => t + 1);
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:    const upgrade = (statName: 'hpLevel' | 'mpLevel' | 'strLevel' | 'mpRegenLevel' | 'speedLevel') => {
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:        const currentLevel = s[statName];
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:        if (currentLevel >= 5) return;
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:        const cost = SKILL_COSTS[currentLevel];
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:        if (s.skillPoints >= cost) {
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:            s.skillPoints -= cost;
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:            s[statName]++;
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:            // apply immediate effects
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:            if (statName === 'hpLevel') {
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:                s.maxHp += 50;
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:                s.hp += 50;
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:            } else if (statName === 'mpLevel') {
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:                s.maxMp += 30;
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:                s.mp += 30;
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:            } else if (statName === 'strLevel') {
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:                s.strength += 3;
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:            } else if (statName === 'mpRegenLevel') {
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:                s.mpRegenBoost += 20;
src/components/SkillTreeUI.tsx:            }

src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:function getNextExp(lvl: number): number {
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:    const baseMobXp = 30;
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:    let mobsNeeded = 3;
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:    switch (lvl) {
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:        case 1: mobsNeeded = 3; break;
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:        case 2: mobsNeeded = 4; break;
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:        case 3: mobsNeeded = 6; break;
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:        case 4: mobsNeeded = 10; break;
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:        case 5: mobsNeeded = 15; break;
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:        default:
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:            // Progressive growth for higher levels
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:            let current = 15;
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:            for (let i = 5; i < lvl; i++) {
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:                current = Math.floor(current * 1.5);
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:            }
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:            mobsNeeded = current;
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:    }
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:    return mobsNeeded * baseMobXp;
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:}
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:function getExpFromMob(mobLevel: number, playerLevel: number, extraXpPct: number = 0): number {
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:    const baseExp = 40;
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:    const diff = mobLevel - playerLevel;
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:    let xp = baseExp;
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:    if (diff > 0) {
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:        xp = Math.floor(baseExp * Math.pow(1.5, diff * 0.5));
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:    } else {
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:        xp = Math.floor(baseExp / Math.max(1, (playerLevel - mobLevel) * 0.2 + 1));
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:    }
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:    return Math.floor(xp * (1 + extraXpPct / 100));
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:}

src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:        const hasLineOfSight = (x1: number, y1: number, x2: number, y2: number) => {
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:            const dist = Math.hypot(x2 - x1, y2 - y1);
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:            const steps = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(dist / 16));
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:            const stepX = (x2 - x1) / steps;
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:            const stepY = (y2 - y1) / steps;
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:            let cx = x1;
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:            let cy = y1;
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:            for (let i = 0; i <= steps; i++) {
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:                if (pointInWall(cx, cy)) return false;
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:                cx += stepX;
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:                cy += stepY;
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:            }
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:            return true;
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:        };

src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:                            drop(g.type, g.name, g.color, { value: g.value, rarityColor: g.rarityColor });
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:                            break;
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:                        }
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:                    }
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:                }
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:            }
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:        }
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:    };
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:    
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:    // Power-based damage calculation helper
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:    const calculateEnemyDamage = (e: Enemy, rawDmg: number, isPlayer: boolean = true, sourceLvl?: number) => {
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:        if (e.hp <= 0) return 0;
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:        
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:        // Base boss resistance
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:        let multiplier = (e.type === 'boss' || e.type === 'slimmy' || e.type === 'serpy' || e.type === 'shadow_reaper') ? 0.5 : 1.0;
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:        if (e.type === 'miniboss') multiplier = 0.7;
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:        // Calculate power ratio
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:        const attackerLvl = isPlayer ? stats.current.lvl : (sourceLvl || 1);
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:        const attackerPower = isPlayer ? (stats.current.lvl + stats.current.strength * 0.5) : attackerLvl;
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:        const enemyLevel = e.level || 1;
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:        
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:        // ratio > 1 means attacker is stronger
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:        const powerRatio = attackerPower / Math.max(1, enemyLevel);
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:        
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:        if (powerRatio > 1.2) {
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:            // Overpowered hero: overcome resistance and boost damage
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:            // We scale up to 3x damage if player is way overleveled
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:            const boost = Math.min(3.0, (powerRatio - 0.2) * 1.5); 
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:            multiplier *= boost;
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:            
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:            // Stagger/Falling logic for bosses
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:            if (isPlayer && (e.type === 'boss' || e.type === 'slimmy' || e.type === 'serpy' || e.type === 'shadow_reaper') && (rawDmg * multiplier > e.maxHp * 0.03)) {
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:                // If hitting for more than 3% of max HP, stagger the boss
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:                e.stunTimer = Math.min(240, (e.stunTimer || 0) + 40); // Boss falls!
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:                
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:                // Visual feedback: many yellow stars
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:                for(let i=0; i<3; i++) {
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:                    particles.current.push({
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:                        x: e.x + (Math.random()-0.5)*e.size,
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:                        y: e.y - e.size,
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:                        vx: (Math.random() - 0.5) * 4, vy: -Math.random() * 5,
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:                        life: 0, maxLife: 30, color: '#ffff00', size: 4
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:                    });
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:                }
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:                
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:                // If very high impact, play extra sound
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:                if (rawDmg * multiplier > e.maxHp * 0.1) {
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:                    audio.playImpactSound();
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:                }
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:            }
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:        } else if (powerRatio < 0.7) {
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:            // Boss is much stronger than player: extreme resistance
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:            const penalty = Math.max(0.05, powerRatio + 0.1);
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:            multiplier *= penalty;
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:        }
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:        return Math.floor(rawDmg * multiplier);
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:    };
src/components/GameCanvas.tsx:
